# Étude fonctionnelle exhaustive graph + dataviz

État : lane DESIGN, couverture inventoriée et affectée ; implémentation et qualification ouvertes. D1=A, D2=C, D3=A, D4=C′, D5=B, D6=C, D7=A appliquées. Les références tierces ne deviennent pas des dépendances runtime.

## 1. Ce qui constitue la preuve

Le catalogue fourni est **augmenté**, avec ses identifiants d’origine conservés. Trois preuves distinctes : `verified-reference` = présence documentaire et données extraites ; `verified-source` = code source présent et recensé ; `verified-requirement` = capacité demandée par le mandat. Aucun de ces états ne signifie `implemented`, conformité normative, ni qualification runtime. Les champs JSON séparent ces axes.

Le périmètre externe est borné par les index officiels, leurs pages détaillées, leur pagination et le sitemap, aux dates de lecture enregistrées par source. « Toutes les bibliothèques au monde » n’est pas un ensemble démontrable ; ici ELK, Graphviz, chaque démo JointJS et toutes les surfaces source Sentropic du mandat ont des identifiants explicites. MSAGL/Dagre/libavoid cités par des démos sont enregistrés comme références supplémentaires ; leur API entière n’est pas assimilée à celle d’ELK.

Sources originales : `.astra-inputs/graph-dataviz-reference-catalog.{md,json}`, source-map, audit DS, inv-graphify-graph, inv-dataviz, mandat et notes owner. Les entrées ont des granularités distinctes (option, algorithme, module, feature, recette) : leur total ne désigne pas autant de fonctions indépendantes. Le JSON compagnon contient une ligne par entrée de couverture, le package propriétaire, les contributeurs, la provenance, les métadonnées, les identités initiales et le rattachement export public→propriétaire canonique. La table aire→package figure dans la spec ; les listes exactes par package figurent dans [package-coverage.md](graph-dataviz-study/package-coverage.md).

| Ensemble énuméré | Total | Preuve / borne |
|---|---|---|
| ELK algorithmes | 21 | Index indépendant = catalogue initial, 21 fiches relues |
| ELK groupes d’options | 60 | Axe séparé ; membres et sous-groupes explicites |
| ELK fiches d’options | 286 | 279 IDs distincts ; 7 fiches supplémentaires de portée fournisseur ; ne pas dédupliquer par ID seul |
| Graphviz moteurs | 10 | Index layouts, auteur de plugins exclu |
| Graphviz attributs | 177 | Tous attributs, types, défauts, usage et restrictions documentées |
| Graphviz sorties | 65 | 64 paramètres index + vt-8up2 sur fiche VT100 ; 36 pages ; formats/variantes/surfaces distingués |
| JointJS vues/démos/apps | 186 | Union /demos et /all-demos paginés = sitemap, zéro écart |
| JointJS features | 61 | Labels officiels individuels, avec correspondance package |
| Sentropic exports source | 3547 | Checker TypeScript = parcours AST indépendant ; 6 packages, 1564 identités canoniques |
| DS composants/helpers graphiques | 93 | 371 couples framework/export runtime au total, React Dashboard absent |
| Entrées de couverture affectées | 1820 | 0 oubli d’une entrée initiale, 0 doublon, 0 package absent |
| Packages du graphe cible | 30 | 18 nouveaux + noms existants conservés ; DAG vérifié |

Les dates UTC exactes, statuts HTTP, URLs finales et SHA-256 sont dans [live-evidence.json](graph-dataviz-study/live-evidence.json). La documentation ELK/Graphviz est mouvante : les versions 0.12.0 / 16.1.0 du catalogue initial sont des repères de release, pas une preuve que chaque page correspond à ces binaires. JointJS core 4.3.3 et JointJS+ n’ont pas une version commune prouvée. Aucun runtime upstream n’a été exécuté. Les écarts source/artefact npm restent `unverified`.

### 1.1 Pagination et rapprochement JointJS

| Page officielle | URL distinctes sur cette page | Suite découverte |
|---|---|---|
| https://www.jointjs.com/demos | 102 | https://www.jointjs.com/demos?7b8f7f41_page=2 |
| https://www.jointjs.com/all-demos | 100 | https://www.jointjs.com/all-demos?8ed441d6_page=2 |
| https://www.jointjs.com/demos?7b8f7f41_page=2 | 109 | https://www.jointjs.com/demos?7b8f7f41_page=1 |
| https://www.jointjs.com/all-demos?8ed441d6_page=2 | 86 | https://www.jointjs.com/all-demos?8ed441d6_page=1 |
| https://www.jointjs.com/demos?7b8f7f41_page=1 | 102 | https://www.jointjs.com/demos?7b8f7f41_page=2 |
| https://www.jointjs.com/all-demos?8ed441d6_page=1 | 100 | https://www.jointjs.com/all-demos?8ed441d6_page=2 |

Les cartes promotionnelles répètent des entrées : les comptes par page ne s’additionnent pas. Les URL sont dédupliquées, puis comparées comme ensembles au sitemap et au catalogue initial. Les 186 fiches sont HTTP 200. **184 destinations embarquées sont accessibles en HTTP ; 2 renvoient 404** (voir gaps). Accessibilité HTTP ne vaut pas exécution. Les 185 fiches comportant une liste de features sont lues intégralement ; `scale-svgmarker` utilise le tag galerie `Custom shapes`, faute de liste sur sa fiche.

Les catégories suivantes sont une **classification analytique Sentropic**, pas des catégories officielles inventées. Les labels galerie supplémentaires sont conservés ; l’appartenance par démo aux tags taxonomiques du site reste source-gap ; `BPMN` dans une carte peut être un tag, pas une 62e feature.

## 2. ELK — algorithmes (axe 1)

Chaque ligne conserve l’ID exact et l’URL. Le libellé « équivalent Sentropic » du catalogue initial reste une piste de réemploi, pas une preuve d’identité algorithmique. Les connecteurs Draw2D/Graphviz/Libavoid ne sont pas présumés exécutables dans elkjs. Toutes les implémentations futures respectent D7=A.

| ID source | Algorithme / capacité | Package canonique | Réemploi constaté / limite | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| org.eclipse.elk.conn.gmf.layouter.Draw2D | Draw2D Layout — Layered du framework Draw2D/GMF | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | Aucun moteur Draw2D/Sugiyama compatible identifié. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-conn-gmf-layouter-Draw2D.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.box | ELK Box — Packing de boîtes sans arêtes | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | graphify hierarchy-aware: shelf packing partiel; grid: cases régulières. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-box.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.disco | ELK DisCo — Placement de composantes déconnectées | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | graphify hierarchy-aware: shelf packing de sous-arbres; aucune équivalence DisCo prouvée. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-disco.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.fixed | ELK Fixed — Conserver positions et points de contrôle fournis | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | graph createStaticLayoutEngine et forceLayout: conservation des positions seulement; pas de contrat ELK complet. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-fixed.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.force | ELK Force — Modèles physiques Eades et Fruchterman–Reingold | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | DS ForceGraph: répulsion O(n²)+ressorts; graphify computeLayout: Barnes–Hut FA2. Modèles et options distincts. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-force.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.layered | ELK Layered — Sugiyama, ports, hiérarchies, routage droit/orthogonal/spline | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | typed-layer groupe les types en lanes; aucun Sugiyama généraliste ni options ELK compatibles établi. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-layered.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.mrtree | ELK Mr. Tree — Arbre couvrant puis disposition hiérarchique | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | graphify hierarchy-aware/radial: géométries de hiérarchie apparentées; pas Mr. Tree. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-mrtree.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.radial | ELK Radial — Arbre radial par niveaux, base Eades | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | graph radial: anneaux BFS; hierarchy-aware: radial tidy-tree. Algorithmes/contraintes différents. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-radial.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.random | ELK Randomizer — Placement aléatoire | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | Seed FNV-1a/mulberry32 dans computeLayout; aucun moteur random public isolé. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-random.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.rectpacking | ELK Rectangle Packing — Packing de rectangles ordonné, approximation largeur/compaction | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | graphify hierarchy-aware shelf packing; DS DashboardGrid modèle de grille, sans équivalence rectpacking. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-rectpacking.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.sporeCompaction | ELK SPOrE Compaction — ShrinkTree: compaction conservant la topologie | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | Compaction git-flow des labels seulement; aucun ShrinkTree généraliste établi. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-sporeCompaction.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.sporeOverlap | ELK SPOrE Overlap Removal — Déchevauchement Grow a Tree | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | Aucun déchevauchement générique Grow a Tree établi. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-sporeOverlap.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.stress | ELK Stress — Stress majorization et distances de graphe | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | Les simulations force Sentropic ne prouvent pas un solveur de stress. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-stress.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.topdownpacking | ELK Top-down Packing — Packing de boîtes et allocation top-down | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | DashboardGrid et shelf packing: capacités adjacentes; pas allocation top-down ELK. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-topdownpacking.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.vertiflex | ELK VertiFlex — Arbre avec positions verticales imposées | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | time-oriented impose une projection temporelle différente; pas VertiFlex. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-vertiflex.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.graphviz.circo | Graphviz Circo — Connecteur ELK vers Graphviz circo | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | radial BFS existe; aucune décomposition bicomposantes Circo établie. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-graphviz-circo.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.graphviz.dot | Graphviz Dot — Connecteur ELK vers Graphviz dot | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | typed-layer ne prouve pas Sugiyama/dot. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-graphviz-dot.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.graphviz.fdp | Graphviz FDP — Connecteur ELK vers Graphviz fdp | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | DS ForceGraph/graphify Barnes–Hut: famille force seulement. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-graphviz-fdp.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.graphviz.neato | Graphviz Neato — Connecteur ELK vers Graphviz neato | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | Aucun stress majorization/Kamada–Kawai établi. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-graphviz-neato.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.graphviz.twopi | Graphviz Twopi — Connecteur ELK vers Graphviz twopi | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | graph radial BFS: principe anneaux-distance apparenté; options/routage distincts. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-graphviz-twopi.html) |
| org.eclipse.elk.alg.libavoid | Libavoid — Connecteur libavoid, routage sans déplacer les nœuds | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | render-geometry: segments/arcs et ports simples; pas évitement générique obstacles. | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/algorithms/org-eclipse-elk-alg-libavoid.html) |

## 3. ELK — groupes et options (axe 2)

### 3.1 Tous les groupes

Un groupe n’est pas un algorithme. Options directes et sous-groupes sont distingués pour éviter les doubles comptes. Les options sont aussi recensées depuis leur index indépendant : les pages référencées par les algorithmes/groupes ne suffisent pas à retrouver les 286 fiches.

### 3.2 Toutes les fiches d’options

Clé de fiche distincte de l’ID : les variantes fournisseur conservent leur page. Domaines/types/défauts proviennent des fiches officielles ; leur transcription n’est pas une qualification des interactions. Les listes de groupes et d’algorithmes supporteurs sont conservées intégralement dans le JSON, ainsi que toute la table de métadonnées source.

| Clé de fiche / ID source | Nom et type | Valeurs / défaut / portée | Package | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| elk-option:org-eclipse-elk-insideSelfLoops-activate / org.eclipse.elk.insideSelfLoops.activate | Activate Inside Self Loops ; boolean | Default Value=false (as defined in org.eclipse.elk ); Applies To=nodes | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/options/org-eclipse-elk-insideSelfLoops-activate.html) |

| elk-option:org-eclipse-elk-validateOptions / org.eclipse.elk.validateOptions | Validate Options ; boolean | Default Value=true (as defined in org.eclipse.elk ); Applies To=parents | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/options/org-eclipse-elk-validateOptions.html) |
| elk-option:org-eclipse-elk-layered-wrapping-validify-strategy / org.eclipse.elk.layered.wrapping.validify.strategy | Validification Strategy ; org.eclipse.elk.alg.layered.options.ValidifyStrategy (Enum) | Possible Values=NO GREEDY LOOK_BACK; Default Value=ValidifyStrategy.GREEDY (as defined in org.eclipse.elk.layered ); Applies To=parents | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/options/org-eclipse-elk-layered-wrapping-validify-strategy.html) |
| elk-option:org-eclipse-elk-spacing-labelPortVertical / org.eclipse.elk.spacing.labelPortVertical | Vertical spacing between Label and Port ; double | Default Value=1 (as defined in org.eclipse.elk ); Applies To=parents | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/options/org-eclipse-elk-spacing-labelPortVertical.html) |
| elk-option:org-eclipse-elk-topdown-sizeCategoriesHierarchicalNodeWeight / org.eclipse.elk.topdown.sizeCategoriesHierarchicalNodeWeight | Weight of a node containing children for determining the graph size ; int | Default Value=4 (as defined in org.eclipse.elk ); Lower Bound=1; Applies To=parents | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/options/org-eclipse-elk-topdown-sizeCategoriesHierarchicalNodeWeight.html) |
| elk-option:org-eclipse-elk-mrtree-weighting / org.eclipse.elk.mrtree.weighting | Weighting of Nodes ; org.eclipse.elk.alg.mrtree.options.OrderWeighting (Enum) | Possible Values=MODEL_ORDER DESCENDANTS FAN CONSTRAINT; Default Value=OrderWeighting.MODEL_ORDER (as defined in org.eclipse.elk.mrtree ); Applies To=parents | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/options/org-eclipse-elk-mrtree-weighting.html) |
| elk-option:org-eclipse-elk-overlapRemoval-runScanline / org.eclipse.elk.overlapRemoval.runScanline | Whether to run a supplementary scanline overlap check. ; boolean | Default Value=true (as defined in org.eclipse.elk ); Applies To=parents | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/options/org-eclipse-elk-overlapRemoval-runScanline.html) |
| elk-option:org-eclipse-elk-rectpacking-whiteSpaceElimination-strategy / org.eclipse.elk.rectpacking.whiteSpaceElimination.strategy | White Space Approximation Strategy ; org.eclipse.elk.alg.rectpacking.p3whitespaceelimination.WhiteSpaceEliminationStrategy (Enum) | Possible Values=EQUAL_BETWEEN_STRUCTURES TO_ASPECT_RATIO NONE; Default Value=WhiteSpaceEliminationStrategy.NONE (as defined in org.eclipse.elk.rectpacking ); Applies To=parents | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/options/org-eclipse-elk-rectpacking-whiteSpaceElimination-strategy.html) |
| elk-option:org-eclipse-elk-topdownpacking-whitespaceElimination-strategy / org.eclipse.elk.topdownpacking.whitespaceElimination.strategy | Whitespace elimination strategy ; org.eclipse.elk.alg.topdownpacking.WhitespaceEliminationStrategy (Enum) | Possible Values=BOTTOM_ROW_EQUAL_WHITESPACE_ELIMINATOR; Default Value=WhitespaceEliminationStrategy.BOTTOM_ROW_EQUAL_WHITESPACE_ELIMINATOR (as defined in org.eclipse.elk.topdownpacking ); Applies To=parents | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/options/org-eclipse-elk-topdownpacking-whitespaceElimination-strategy.html) |
| elk-option:org-eclipse-elk-rectpacking-widthApproximation-strategy / org.eclipse.elk.rectpacking.widthApproximation.strategy | Width Approximation Strategy ; org.eclipse.elk.alg.rectpacking.p1widthapproximation.WidthApproximationStrategy (Enum) | Possible Values=GREEDY TARGET_WIDTH; Default Value=WidthApproximationStrategy.GREEDY (as defined in org.eclipse.elk.rectpacking ); Applies To=parents | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/options/org-eclipse-elk-rectpacking-widthApproximation-strategy.html) |
| elk-option:org-eclipse-elk-zoomToFit / org.eclipse.elk.zoomToFit | Zoom to Fit ; boolean | Default Value=false (as defined in org.eclipse.elk ); Applies To=parents | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://eclipse.dev/elk/reference/options/org-eclipse-elk-zoomToFit.html) |

## 4. Graphviz — moteurs, attributs, sorties

### 4.1 Tous les moteurs/layouts

| ID | Capacité | Package | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| dot | Hiérarchique/layered, orienté, minimisation croisements | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/layouts/dot/) |
| neato | Stress majorization; variante historique Kamada–Kawai | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/layouts/neato/) |
| fdp | Fruchterman–Reingold, multigrille, clusters | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/layouts/fdp/) |
| sfdp | Force multi-échelle pour grands graphes | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/layouts/sfdp/) |
| circo | Disposition circulaire de structures cycliques/bicomposantes | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/layouts/circo/) |
| twopi | Anneaux selon distance à une racine | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/layouts/twopi/) |
| nop | Normalisation/pretty-print DOT; alias nop1 | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/layouts/nop/) |
| nop2 | Positions déjà connues, équivalent invocation neato -n2 | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/layouts/nop2/) |
| osage | Packing récursif de clusters puis routage | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/layouts/osage/) |
| patchwork | Treemap squarifié de clusters pondérés | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/layouts/patchwork/) |

### 4.2 Tous les attributs

Les 177 attributs sont conservés, même ceux spécifiques au rendu, à une plateforme ou à l’export. Le parser DOT appartient à `graph-codecs` ; la colonne package désigne le propriétaire du **comportement**. Un attribut reconnu mais non implémenté doit être conservé ou diagnostiqué, jamais silencieusement accepté comme fonctionnel. L’absence de restriction explicite dans la table ne prouve pas que tous les moteurs l’appliquent.

| Attribut | Usage ; type ; défaut ; min | Fonction / restriction documentée | Package | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| _background | used_by=Graphs; type=xdot; default=&lt;none&gt;; minimum= | A string in the xdot format specifying an arbitrary background. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/background/) |
| area | used_by=Nodes, Clusters; type=double; default=1.0; minimum=&gt;0 | Indicates the preferred area for a node or empty cluster. patchwork only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/area/) |
| arrowhead | used_by=Edges; type=arrowType; default=normal; minimum= | Style of arrowhead on the head node of an edge. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/arrowhead/) |
| arrowsize | used_by=Edges; type=double; default=1.0; minimum=0.0 | Multiplicative scale factor for arrowheads. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/arrowsize/) |
| arrowtail | used_by=Edges; type=arrowType; default=normal; minimum= | Style of arrowhead on the tail node of an edge. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/arrowtail/) |
| bb | used_by=Clusters, Graphs; type=rect; default=; minimum= | Bounding box of drawing in points. write only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/bb/) |
| beautify | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | Whether to draw leaf nodes uniformly in a circle around the root node in sfdp.. sfdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/beautify/) |
| bgcolor | used_by=Graphs, Clusters; type=color , colorList; default=&lt;none&gt;; minimum= | Canvas background color. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/bgcolor/) |
| center | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | Whether to center the drawing in the output canvas. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/center/) |
| charset | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default="UTF-8"; minimum= | Character encoding used when interpreting string input as a text label.. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/charset/) |
| class | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Clusters, Graphs; type=string; default=""; minimum= | Classnames to attach to the node, edge, graph, or cluster's SVG element. svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/class/) |
| cluster | used_by=Clusters, Subgraphs; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | Whether the subgraph is a cluster. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/cluster/) |
| clusterrank | used_by=Graphs; type=clusterMode; default=local; minimum= | Mode used for handling clusters. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/clusterrank/) |
| color | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Clusters; type=color , colorList; default=black; minimum= | Basic drawing color for graphics, not text. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/color/) |
| colorscheme | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Clusters, Graphs; type=string; default=""; minimum= | A color scheme namespace: the context for interpreting color names. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/colorscheme/) |
| comment | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Graphs; type=string; default=""; minimum= | Comments are inserted into output. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/comment/) |
| compound | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | If true, allow edges between clusters. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/compound/) |
| concentrate | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | If true, use edge concentrators. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/concentrate/) |
| constraint | used_by=Edges; type=bool; default=true; minimum= | If false, the edge is not used in ranking the nodes. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/constraint/) |
| Damping | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=0.99; minimum=0.0 | Factor damping force motions.. neato only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/Damping/) |
| decorate | used_by=Edges; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | Whether to connect the edge label to the edge with a line. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/decorate/) |
| defaultdist | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=1+(avg. len)*sqrt(\|V\|); minimum=epsilon | The distance between nodes in separate connected components. neato only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/defaultdist/) |
| dim | used_by=Graphs; type=int; default=2; minimum=2 | Set the number of dimensions used for the layout. neato , fdp , sfdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/dim/) |
| dimen | used_by=Graphs; type=int; default=2; minimum=2 | Set the number of dimensions used for rendering. neato , fdp , sfdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/dimen/) |
| dir | used_by=Edges; type=dirType; default=forward (directed) none (undirected); minimum= | Edge type for drawing arrowheads. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/dir/) |
| diredgeconstraints | used_by=Graphs; type=string , bool; default=false; minimum= | Whether to constrain most edges to point downwards. neato only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/diredgeconstraints/) |
| distortion | used_by=Nodes; type=double; default=0.0; minimum=-100.0 | Distortion factor for shape = polygon . | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/distortion/) |
| dpi | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=96.0; minimum=0.0 | Specifies the expected number of pixels per inch on a display device. bitmap output, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/dpi/) |
| edgehref | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | Synonym for edgeURL . map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/edgehref/) |
| edgetarget | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=&lt;none&gt;; minimum= | Browser window to use for the edgeURL link. map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/edgetarget/) |
| edgetooltip | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | Tooltip annotation attached to the non-label part of an edge. cmap , svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/edgetooltip/) |
| edgeURL | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | The link for the non-label parts of an edge. map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/edgeURL/) |
| epsilon | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=.0001 * # nodes (mode == KK) .0001 (mode == major) .01 (mode == sgd); minimum= | Terminating condition. neato only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/epsilon/) |
| esep | used_by=Graphs; type=addDouble , addPoint; default=+3; minimum= | Margin used around polygons for purposes of spline edge routing. neato , fdp , sfdp , osage, circo , twopi only. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/esep/) |
| fillcolor | used_by=Nodes, Edges, Clusters; type=color , colorList; default=lightgrey (nodes) black (clusters); minimum= | Color used to fill the background of a node or cluster. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/fillcolor/) |
| fixedsize | used_by=Nodes; type=bool , string; default=false; minimum= | Whether to use the specified width and height attributes to choose node size (rather than sizing to fit the node contents). | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/fixedsize/) |
| fontcolor | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Graphs, Clusters; type=color; default=black; minimum= | Color used for text. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/fontcolor/) |
| fontname | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Graphs, Clusters; type=string; default="Times-Roman"; minimum= | Font used for text. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/fontname/) |
| fontnames | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default=""; minimum= | Allows user control of how basic fontnames are represented in SVG output. svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/fontnames/) |
| fontpath | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default=&lt;system-dependent&gt;; minimum= | Directory list used by libgd to search for bitmap fonts. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/fontpath/) |
| fontsize | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Graphs, Clusters; type=double; default=14.0; minimum=1.0 | Font size, in points , used for text. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/fontsize/) |
| forcelabels | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=true; minimum= | Whether to force placement of all xlabels , even if overlapping. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/forcelabels/) |
| gradientangle | used_by=Nodes, Clusters, Graphs; type=int; default=0; minimum=0 | If a gradient fill is being used, this determines the angle of the fill. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/gradientangle/) |
| group | used_by=Nodes; type=string; default=""; minimum= | Name for a group of nodes, for bundling edges avoiding crossings.. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/group/) |
| head_lp | used_by=Edges; type=point; default=; minimum= | Center position of an edge's head label. write only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/head_lp/) |
| headclip | used_by=Edges; type=bool; default=true; minimum= | If true, the head of an edge is clipped to the boundary of the head node. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/headclip/) |
| headhref | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | Synonym for headURL . map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/headhref/) |
| headlabel | used_by=Edges; type=lblString; default=""; minimum= | Text label to be placed near head of edge. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/headlabel/) |
| headport | used_by=Edges; type=portPos; default=center; minimum= | Indicates where on the head node to attach the head of the edge. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/headport/) |
| headtarget | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=&lt;none&gt;; minimum= | Browser window to use for the headURL link. map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/headtarget/) |
| headtooltip | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | Tooltip annotation attached to the head of an edge. cmap , svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/headtooltip/) |
| headURL | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | If defined, headURL is output as part of the head label of the edge. map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/headURL/) |
| height | used_by=Nodes; type=double; default=0.5; minimum=0.02 | Height of node, in inches. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/height/) |
| href | used_by=Graphs, Clusters, Nodes, Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | Synonym for URL . map, postscript , svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/href/) |
| id | used_by=Graphs, Clusters, Nodes, Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | Identifier for graph objects. map, postscript , svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/id/) |
| image | used_by=Nodes; type=string; default=""; minimum= | Gives the name of a file containing an image to be displayed inside a node. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/image/) |
| imagepath | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default=""; minimum= | A list of directories in which to look for image files. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/imagepath/) |
| imagepos | used_by=Nodes; type=string; default="mc"; minimum= | Controls how an image is positioned within its containing node. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/imagepos/) |
| imagescale | used_by=Nodes; type=bool , string; default=false; minimum= | Controls how an image fills its containing node. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/imagescale/) |
| inputscale | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=&lt;none&gt;; minimum= | Scales the input positions to convert between length units. neato , fdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/inputscale/) |
| K | used_by=Graphs, Clusters; type=double; default=0.3; minimum=0 | Spring constant used in virtual physical model. fdp , sfdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/K/) |
| label | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Graphs, Clusters; type=lblString; default="\N" (nodes) "" (otherwise); minimum= | Text label attached to objects. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/label/) |
| label_scheme | used_by=Graphs; type=int; default=0; minimum=0 | Whether to treat a node whose name has the form \|edgelabel\|* as a special node representing an edge label.. sfdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/label_scheme/) |
| labelangle | used_by=Edges; type=double; default=-25.0; minimum=-180.0 | The angle (in degrees) in polar coordinates of the head & tail edge labels.. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labelangle/) |
| labeldistance | used_by=Edges; type=double; default=1.0; minimum=0.0 | Scaling factor for the distance of headlabel / taillabel from the head / tail nodes.. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labeldistance/) |
| labelfloat | used_by=Edges; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | If true, allows edge labels to be less constrained in position. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labelfloat/) |
| labelfontcolor | used_by=Edges; type=color; default=black; minimum= | Color used for headlabel and taillabel .. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labelfontcolor/) |
| labelfontname | used_by=Edges; type=string; default="Times-Roman"; minimum= | Font for headlabel and taillabel . | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labelfontname/) |
| labelfontsize | used_by=Edges; type=double; default=14.0; minimum=1.0 | Font size of headlabel and taillabel . | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labelfontsize/) |
| labelhref | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | Synonym for labelURL . map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labelhref/) |
| labeljust | used_by=Graphs, Clusters; type=string; default="c"; minimum= | Justification for graph & cluster labels. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labeljust/) |
| labelloc | used_by=Nodes, Graphs, Clusters; type=string; default="t" (clusters) "b" (root graphs) "c" (nodes); minimum= | Vertical placement of labels for nodes, root graphs and clusters. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labelloc/) |
| labeltarget | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=&lt;none&gt;; minimum= | Browser window to open labelURL links in. map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labeltarget/) |
| labeltooltip | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | Tooltip annotation attached to label of an edge. cmap , svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labeltooltip/) |
| labelURL | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | If defined, labelURL is the link used for the label of an edge. map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/labelURL/) |
| landscape | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | If true, the graph is rendered in landscape mode. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/landscape/) |
| layer | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Clusters; type=layerRange; default=""; minimum= | Specifies layers in which the node, edge or cluster is present. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/layer/) |
| layerlistsep | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default=","; minimum= | The separator characters used to split attributes of type layerRange into a list of ranges.. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/layerlistsep/) |
| layers | used_by=Graphs; type=layerList; default=""; minimum= | A linearly ordered list of layer names attached to the graph. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/layers/) |
| layerselect | used_by=Graphs; type=layerRange; default=""; minimum= | Selects a list of layers to be emitted. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/layerselect/) |
| layersep | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default=":\t "; minimum= | The separator characters for splitting the layers attribute into a list of layer names.. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/layersep/) |
| layout | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default=""; minimum= | Which layout engine to use. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/layout/) |
| len | used_by=Edges; type=double; default=1.0 (neato) 0.3 (fdp); minimum= | Preferred edge length, in inches. neato , fdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/len/) |
| levels | used_by=Graphs; type=int; default=INT_MAX; minimum=0.0 | Number of levels allowed in the multilevel scheme. sfdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/levels/) |
| levelsgap | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=0.0; minimum= | strictness of neato level constraints. neato only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/levelsgap/) |
| lhead | used_by=Edges; type=string; default=""; minimum= | Logical head of an edge. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/lhead/) |
| lheight | used_by=Graphs, Clusters; type=double; default=; minimum= | Height of graph or cluster label, in inches. write only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/lheight/) |
| linelength | used_by=Graphs; type=int; default=128; minimum=60 | How long strings should get before overflowing to next line, for text output.. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/linelength/) |
| lp | used_by=Edges, Graphs, Clusters; type=point; default=; minimum= | Label center position. write only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/lp/) |
| ltail | used_by=Edges; type=string; default=""; minimum= | Logical tail of an edge. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/ltail/) |
| lwidth | used_by=Graphs, Clusters; type=double; default=; minimum= | Width of graph or cluster label, in inches. write only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/lwidth/) |
| margin | used_by=Nodes, Clusters, Graphs; type=double , point; default=&lt;device-dependent&gt;; minimum= | For graphs, this sets x and y margins of canvas, in inches. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/margin/) |
| maxiter | used_by=Graphs; type=int; default=100 * # nodes (mode == KK) 200 (mode == major) 30 (mode == sgd) 600 (fdp); minimum= | Sets the number of iterations used. neato , fdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/maxiter/) |
| mclimit | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=1.0; minimum= | Scale factor for mincross (mc) edge crossing minimizer parameters. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/mclimit/) |
| mindist | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=1.0; minimum=0.0 | Specifies the minimum separation between all nodes. circo only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/mindist/) |
| minlen | used_by=Edges; type=int; default=1; minimum=0 | Minimum edge length (rank difference between head and tail). dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/minlen/) |
| mode | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default=major; minimum= | Technique for optimizing the layout. neato only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/mode/) |
| model | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default=shortpath; minimum= | Specifies how the distance matrix is computed for the input graph. neato only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/model/) |
| newrank | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | Whether to use a single global ranking, ignoring clusters. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/newrank/) |
| nodesep | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=0.25; minimum=0.02 | In dot , nodesep specifies the minimum space between two adjacent nodes in the same rank, in inches. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/nodesep/) |
| nojustify | used_by=Graphs, Clusters, Nodes, Edges; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | Whether to justify multiline text vs the previous text line (rather than the side of the container).. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/nojustify/) |
| normalize | used_by=Graphs; type=double , bool; default=false; minimum= | normalizes coordinates of final layout. neato , fdp , sfdp , twopi , circo only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/normalize/) |
| notranslate | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | Whether to avoid translating layout to the origin point. neato only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/notranslate/) |
| nslimit | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=; minimum= | Sets number of iterations in network simplex applications. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/nslimit/) |
| nslimit1 | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=; minimum= | Sets number of iterations in network simplex applications. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/nslimit1/) |
| oneblock | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | Whether to draw circo graphs around one circle.. circo only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/oneblock/) |
| ordering | used_by=Graphs, Nodes; type=string; default=""; minimum= | Constrains the left-to-right ordering of node edges.. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/ordering/) |
| orientation | used_by=Nodes, Graphs; type=double , string; default=0.0 ""; minimum=-360.0 | node shape rotation angle, or graph orientation. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/orientation/) |
| outputorder | used_by=Graphs; type=outputMode; default=breadthfirst; minimum= | Specify order in which nodes and edges are drawn. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/outputorder/) |
| overlap | used_by=Graphs; type=string , bool; default=true; minimum= | Determines if and how node overlaps should be removed. fdp , neato , sfdp , circo , twopi only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/overlap/) |
| overlap_scaling | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=-4; minimum=-1e+10 | Scale layout by factor, to reduce node overlap.. prism , neato , sfdp , fdp , circo , twopi only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/overlap_scaling/) |
| overlap_shrink | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=true; minimum= | Whether the overlap removal algorithm should perform a compression pass to reduce the size of the layout. prism only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/overlap_shrink/) |
| pack | used_by=Graphs; type=bool , int; default=false; minimum= | Whether each connected component of the graph should be laid out separately, and then the graphs packed together.. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/pack/) |
| packmode | used_by=Graphs; type=packMode; default=node; minimum= | How connected components should be packed. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/packmode/) |
| pad | used_by=Graphs; type=double , point; default=0.0555 (4 points); minimum= | Inches to extend the drawing area around the minimal area needed to draw the graph. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/pad/) |
| page | used_by=Graphs; type=double , point; default=; minimum= | Width and height of output pages, in inches. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/page/) |
| pagedir | used_by=Graphs; type=pagedir; default=BL; minimum= | The order in which pages are emitted. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/pagedir/) |
| pencolor | used_by=Clusters; type=color; default=black; minimum= | Color used to draw the bounding box around a cluster. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/pencolor/) |
| penwidth | used_by=Clusters, Nodes, Edges; type=double; default=1.0; minimum=0.0 | Specifies the width of the pen, in points, used to draw lines and curves. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/penwidth/) |
| peripheries | used_by=Nodes, Clusters; type=int; default=&lt;shape default&gt; (nodes) 1 (clusters); minimum=0 | Set number of peripheries used in polygonal shapes and cluster boundaries. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/peripheries/) |
| pin | used_by=Nodes; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | Keeps the node at the node's given input position. neato , fdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/pin/) |
| pos | used_by=Edges, Nodes; type=point , splineType; default=; minimum= | Position of node, or spline control points. neato , fdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/pos/) |
| quadtree | used_by=Graphs; type=quadType , bool; default=normal; minimum= | Quadtree scheme to use. sfdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/quadtree/) |
| quantum | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=0.0; minimum=0.0 | If quantum &gt; 0.0 , node label dimensions will be rounded to integral multiples of the quantum. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/quantum/) |
| radius | used_by=Edges; type=double; default=0; minimum=0 | Radius of rounded corners on orthogonal edges. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/radius/) |
| rank | used_by=Subgraphs; type=rankType; default=; minimum= | Rank constraints on the nodes in a subgraph. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/rank/) |
| rankdir | used_by=Graphs; type=rankdir; default=TB; minimum= | Sets direction of graph layout. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/rankdir/) |
| ranksep | used_by=Graphs; type=double , doubleList; default=0.5 (dot) 1.0 (twopi); minimum=0.02 | Specifies separation between ranks. dot , twopi only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/ranksep/) |
| ratio | used_by=Graphs; type=double , string; default=; minimum= | Sets the aspect ratio (drawing height/drawing width) for the drawing. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/ratio/) |
| rects | used_by=Nodes; type=rect; default=; minimum= | Rectangles for fields of records, in points . write only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/rects/) |
| regular | used_by=Nodes; type=bool; default=false; minimum= | If true, force polygon to be regular.. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/regular/) |
| remincross | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=true; minimum= | If there are multiple clusters, whether to run edge crossing minimization a second time.. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/remincross/) |
| repulsiveforce | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=1.0; minimum=0.0 | The power of the repulsive force used in an extended Fruchterman-Reingold. sfdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/repulsiveforce/) |
| resolution | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=96.0; minimum=0.0 | Synonym for dpi .. bitmap output, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/resolution/) |
| root | used_by=Graphs, Nodes; type=string , bool; default=&lt;none&gt; (graphs) false (nodes); minimum= | Specifies nodes to be used as the center of the layout. twopi , circo only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/root/) |
| rotate | used_by=Graphs; type=int; default=0; minimum= | If rotate=90 , sets drawing orientation to landscape. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/rotate/) |
| rotation | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=0; minimum= | Rotates the final layout counter-clockwise by the specified number of degrees. sfdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/rotation/) |
| samehead | used_by=Edges; type=string; default=""; minimum= | Edges with the same head and the same samehead value are aimed at the same point on the head. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/samehead/) |
| sametail | used_by=Edges; type=string; default=""; minimum= | Edges with the same tail and the same sametail value are aimed at the same point on the tail.. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/sametail/) |
| samplepoints | used_by=Nodes; type=int; default=8 (output) 20 (overlap and image maps); minimum= | Gives the number of points used for a circle/ellipse node. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/samplepoints/) |
| scale | used_by=Graphs; type=double , point; default=; minimum= | Scales layout by the given factor after the initial layout. neato , twopi only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/scale/) |
| searchsize | used_by=Graphs; type=int; default=30; minimum= | During network simplex, the maximum number of edges with negative cut values to search when looking for an edge with minimum cut value.. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/searchsize/) |
| sep | used_by=Graphs; type=addDouble , addPoint; default=+4; minimum= | Margin to leave around nodes when removing node overlap. fdp , neato , sfdp , osage, circo , twopi only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/sep/) |
| shape | used_by=Nodes; type=shape; default=ellipse; minimum= | Sets the shape of a node. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/shape/) |
| shapefile | used_by=Nodes; type=string; default=""; minimum= | A file containing user-supplied node content. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/shapefile/) |
| showboxes | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Graphs; type=int; default=0; minimum=0 | Print guide boxes for debugging. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/showboxes/) |
| sides | used_by=Nodes; type=int; default=4; minimum=0 | Number of sides when shape =polygon . | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/sides/) |
| size | used_by=Graphs; type=double , point; default=; minimum= | Maximum width and height of drawing, in inches. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/size/) |
| skew | used_by=Nodes; type=double; default=0.0; minimum=-100.0 | Skew factor for shape =polygon . | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/skew/) |
| smoothing | used_by=Graphs; type=smoothType; default="none"; minimum= | Specifies a post-processing step used to smooth out an uneven distribution of nodes.. sfdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/smoothing/) |
| sortv | used_by=Graphs, Clusters, Nodes; type=int; default=0; minimum=0 | Sort order of graph components for ordering packmode packing.. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/sortv/) |
| splines | used_by=Graphs; type=bool , string; default=; minimum= | Controls how, and if, edges are represented. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/splines/) |
| start | used_by=Graphs; type=startType; default=""; minimum= | Parameter used to determine the initial layout of nodes. neato , fdp , sfdp only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/start/) |
| style | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Clusters, Graphs; type=style; default=""; minimum= | Set style information for components of the graph. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/style/) |
| stylesheet | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default=""; minimum= | A URL or pathname specifying an XML style sheet, used in SVG output. svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/stylesheet/) |
| tail_lp | used_by=Edges; type=point; default=; minimum= | Position of an edge's tail label, in points .. write only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/tail_lp/) |
| tailclip | used_by=Edges; type=bool; default=true; minimum= | If true, the tail of an edge is clipped to the boundary of the tail node. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/tailclip/) |
| tailhref | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | Synonym for tailURL .. map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/tailhref/) |
| taillabel | used_by=Edges; type=lblString; default=""; minimum= | Text label to be placed near tail of edge. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/taillabel/) |
| tailport | used_by=Edges; type=portPos; default=center; minimum= | Indicates where on the tail node to attach the tail of the edge. | `@sentropic/graph-routing` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/tailport/) |
| tailtarget | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=&lt;none&gt;; minimum= | Browser window to use for the tailURL link. map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/tailtarget/) |
| tailtooltip | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | Tooltip annotation attached to the tail of an edge. cmap , svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/tailtooltip/) |
| tailURL | used_by=Edges; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | If defined, tailURL is output as part of the tail label of the edge. map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/tailURL/) |
| target | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Graphs, Clusters; type=escString , string; default=&lt;none&gt;; minimum= | If the object has a URL , this attribute determines which window of the browser is used for the URL.. map, svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/target/) |
| TBbalance | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default=''; minimum= | Which rank to move floating (loose) nodes to. dot only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/TBbalance/) |
| tooltip | used_by=Nodes, Edges, Clusters, Graphs; type=escString; default=""; minimum= | Tooltip (mouse hover text) attached to the node, edge, cluster, or graph. cmap , svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/tooltip/) |
| truecolor | used_by=Graphs; type=bool; default=; minimum= | Whether internal bitmap rendering relies on a truecolor color model or uses. bitmap output only. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/truecolor/) |
| URL | used_by=Edges, Nodes, Graphs, Clusters; type=escString; default=&lt;none&gt;; minimum= | Hyperlinks incorporated into device-dependent output. map, postscript , svg only. | `@sentropic/graph-svg` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/URL/) |
| vertices | used_by=Nodes; type=pointList; default=; minimum= | Sets the coordinates of the vertices of the node's polygon, in inches. write only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/vertices/) |
| viewport | used_by=Graphs; type=viewPort; default=""; minimum= | Clipping window on final drawing. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/viewport/) |
| voro_margin | used_by=Graphs; type=double; default=0.05; minimum=0.0 | Tuning margin of Voronoi technique. neato , fdp , sfdp , twopi , circo only. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/voro_margin/) |
| weight | used_by=Edges; type=int , double; default=1; minimum=0(dot,twopi) 1(neato,fdp) | Weight of edge. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/weight/) |
| width | used_by=Nodes; type=double; default=0.75; minimum=0.01 | Width of node, in inches. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/width/) |
| xdotversion | used_by=Graphs; type=string; default=; minimum= | Determines the version of xdot used in output. xdot only. | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/xdotversion/) |
| xlabel | used_by=Edges, Nodes; type=lblString; default=""; minimum= | External label for a node or edge. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/xlabel/) |
| xlp | used_by=Nodes, Edges; type=point; default=; minimum= | Position of an exterior label, in points . write only. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/xlp/) |
| z | used_by=Nodes; type=double; default=0.0; minimum= | Z-coordinate value for 3D layouts and displays. | `@sentropic/graph-scene` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/attrs/z/) |

### 4.3 Tous les paramètres de sortie

Un paramètre n’est pas toujours un format de fichier : GTK/X11/CGImage sont des surfaces natives ; Kitty/VT des protocoles terminal ; `ps2` est du PostScript annoté, pas un encodeur PDF. `vml/vmlz` sont documentés mais retirés upstream. Tous ont un propriétaire de capacité, sans prétendre les implémenter ni importer leurs librairies natives. SVG/PNG/PDF conservent en plus leur voie Sentropic actuelle, distincte de la compatibilité Graphviz.

| Paramètre | Famille et nature | Statut référence | Package | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ascii | ascii ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/ascii/) |
| bmp | bmp ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/bmp/) |
| cgimage | cgimage ; interactive-surface | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/cgimage/) |
| canon | canon ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/canon/) |
| dot | canon ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/canon/) |
| gv | canon ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/canon/) |
| xdot | canon ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/canon/) |
| xdot1.2 | canon ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/canon/) |
| xdot1.4 | canon ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/canon/) |
| eps | eps ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/eps/) |
| exr | exr ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/exr/) |
| fig | fig ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/fig/) |
| gd | gd ; file-format-or-variant | documented reference; capability absent in Sentropic until qualified | `@sentropic/graph-export` | [source](https://graphviz.org/docs/outputs/gd/) |

## 5. Moteurs, calculs et surfaces réellement présents chez Sentropic

### 5.1 Moteurs et orchestration identifiés

`forceLayout` transmet les positions ; le calcul Barnes–Hut se trouve dans `graphify/src/graph-layout.ts`. `buildForceGraphData` transforme les données, sans résoudre la simulation. Un worker exécute un calcul ; une sélection de layout dans `scene-layout.ts` reste un raccord consommateur. Les copies DS sont inventoriées comme sources à réutiliser et à faire déléguer, pas comme nouveaux solveurs concurrents.

| ID | Fonction constatée | Package canonique cible | Source |
|---|---|---|---|
| sentropic-current:graph-registry-force | forceLayout — Passthrough: copie positions précalculées, aucune simulation. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://github.com/rhanka/graphify/blob/8f19554cea3a4397fd66a90c7e9fd5ea54c81626/packages/graph/src/layout-registry.ts) |
| sentropic-current:graph-static | createStaticLayoutEngine — Frame statique. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://github.com/rhanka/graphify/blob/8f19554cea3a4397fd66a90c7e9fd5ea54c81626/packages/graph/src/layout.ts) |
| sentropic-current:graph-typed-layer | computeTypedLayerPositions — Lanes par type déterministes; pas Sugiyama. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://github.com/rhanka/graphify/blob/8f19554cea3a4397fd66a90c7e9fd5ea54c81626/packages/graph/src/layout-registry.ts) |
| sentropic-current:graph-time-oriented | computeTimeOrientedPositions — Projection temporelle + lanes par type. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://github.com/rhanka/graphify/blob/8f19554cea3a4397fd66a90c7e9fd5ea54c81626/packages/graph/src/layout-registry.ts) |
| sentropic-current:graph-git-flow | computeGitFlowPositions — Placement commits/branches/lanes; producteur métier reste graphify. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://github.com/rhanka/graphify/blob/8f19554cea3a4397fd66a90c7e9fd5ea54c81626/packages/graph/src/layout-gitflow.ts) |
| sentropic-current:graph-radial | computeRadialPositions — Anneaux BFS depuis le plus haut degré. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://github.com/rhanka/graphify/blob/8f19554cea3a4397fd66a90c7e9fd5ea54c81626/packages/graph/src/layout-radial.ts) |
| sentropic-current:graph-grid | computeGridPositions — Grille déterministe. | `@sentropic/graph-layout` | [source](https://github.com/rhanka/graphify/blob/8f19554cea3a4397fd66a90c7e9fd5ea54c81626/packages/graph/src/layout-grid.ts) |
